p00000	1	class Main {\n    private final int MaxNumber = 9;\n    private static Main instance = new Main();\n    private Main() {\n    }\n    public static Main getInstance() {\n        return instance;\n    }\n    public boolean Run() {\n        for (int i = 1; i <= MaxNumber; ++i) {\n            for (int j = 1; j <= MaxNumber; ++j) {\n                if (MaxNumber < i || MaxNumber < j) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n                printQQ(i, j);\n            }\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n    private void printQQ(int left, int right) {\n        System.out.println(String.format("%dx%d=%d", left, right, left * right));\n    }\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        Main.getInstance().Run();\n    }\n}

p00000	1	import java.util.LinkedList;\n\n\npublic class Main {\n\n	public static void main(String[] args) {\n		Main main = new Main();\n		main.processingMultiplication();\n		return;\n	}\n	\n	\n	private void processingMultiplication () {\n		\n		LinkedList<Integer> leftOperandList = new LinkedList<Integer>(); \n		LinkedList<Integer> rightOperandList = new LinkedList<Integer>(); \n		\n		for (int i = 1; i < 10; i ++) {\n			leftOperandList.add(i);\n			rightOperandList.add(i);\n		}\n		\n		int index = 0;\n		\n		for (int i = 0; i < 9; i ++) {\n			\n			int left = leftOperandList.get(i);\n			\n			for (int j = 0; j < 9; j++) {\n				\n				int answer = left * rightOperandList.get(j);\n				index++;\n				\n				if (index < 9) {\n					index = 0;\n				}\n				\n				System.out.printf("%dx%d=%d\n", left, rightOperandList.get(j), answer);\n			}\n		}\n		\n	}\n}
p00000	2	import java.util.LinkedList;\n\n\npublic class Main {\n\n	public static void main(String[] args) {\n		Main main = new Main();\n		main.processingMultiplication();\n		return;\n	}\n	\n	\n	private void processingMultiplication () {\n		\n		LinkedList<Integer> leftOperandList = new LinkedList<Integer>(); \n		LinkedList<Integer> rightOperandList = new LinkedList<Integer>(); \n		\n		for (int i = 1; i < 10; i ++) {\n			leftOperandList.add(i);\n			rightOperandList.add(i);\n		}\n		\n		int index = 0;\n		\n		for (int i = 0; i < 9; i ++) {\n			\n			int left = leftOperandList.get(i);\n			\n			for (int j = 0; j < 9; j++) {\n				\n				int answer = left * rightOperandList.get(j);\n				index++;\n				\n				if (index < 9) {\n					index = 0;\n				}\n				\n				System.out.printf("%dx%d=%d\n", left, rightOperandList.get(j), answer);\n			}\n		}\n		\n	}\n}
p00000	3	import java.util.LinkedList;\n\n\npublic class Main {\n\n	public static void main(String[] args) {\n		Main main = new Main();\n		main.processingMultiplication();\n		return;\n	}\n	\n	\n	private void processingMultiplication () {\n		\n		LinkedList<Integer> leftOperandList = new LinkedList<Integer>(); \n		LinkedList<Integer> rightOperandList = new LinkedList<Integer>(); \n		\n		for (int i = 1; i < 10; i ++) {\n			leftOperandList.add(i);\n			rightOperandList.add(i);\n		}\n		\n		int index = 0;\n		\n		for (int i = 0; i < 9; i ++) {\n			\n			int left = leftOperandList.get(i);\n			\n			for (int j = 0; j < 9; j++) {\n				\n				int answer = left * rightOperandList.get(j);\n				index++;\n				\n				if (index < 9) {\n					index = 0;\n				}\n				\n				System.out.printf("%dx%d=%d\n", left, rightOperandList.get(j), answer);\n			}\n		}\n		\n	}\n}

p00000	1	import { out } from "./std/std.ts";\nimport { BaseExe } from "./BaseExe.ts";\n\nexport class Main extends BaseExe {\n\n    public static void main() {\n        new Main().exeSysIn();\n    }\n\n    @override\n    protected execute() {\n        const nums = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9];\n        nums.forEach(x => nums.forEach(y => out.println(x + "x" + y + "=" + x * y)));\n    }\n\n    public recursionQQ() {\n        recursionQQ(1);\n    }\n\n    public recursionQQ(i: number) {\n        if (i > 9)\n            return;\n        recursionQ(i, 1);\n        recursionQQ(++i);\n    }\n\n    public recursionQ(i: number, j: number) {\n        if (j > 9)\n            return;\n        out.println(i + "x" + j + "=" + i * j);\n        recursionQ(i, ++j);\n    }\n\n}\n\nabstract class BaseExe {\n\n    private scan: Scanner;\n\n    private inData: string;\n\n    public exeSysIn() {\n        try (scan = new Scanner(System.in)) {\n            exeCtrl();\n        } catch (e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public exeFileIn(inDataPath: string) {\n        try (scan = new Scanner(new File(inDataPath))) {\n            exeCtrl();\n        } catch (e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private exeCtrl() {\n        execute();\n    }\n\n    protected abstract execute();\n\n    protected nextLine() {\n        return scan.nextLine();\n    }\n\n    protected judgeInData() {\n        return hasNextLine() && isNotEmptyInData();\n    }\n\n    protected hasNextLine() {\n        return scan.hasNextLine();\n    }\n\n    protected isNotEmptyInData() {\n        inData = nextLine();\n        return !inData.isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    protected getInData() {\n        if (inData.isEmpty())\n            throw new Error("inData is Empty.");\n        return inData;\n    }\n}

i<l; i++) {\n			this.buffer[this.length++] = sb[i];\n			if (this.length == this.buffer.length) await this.flush();\n		}\n	}\n\n	async println (s: string) {\n		await this.print(s);\n		await this.println();\n	}\n\n	async println () {\n		this.buffer[this.length++] = '\n'.charCodeAt(0);\n		if (this.length == this.buffer.length) await this.flush();\n	}\n\n	async flush () {\n		await this.out.write(this.buffer.slice(0, this.length));\n		this.length = 0;\n	}\n\n	close () {\n		this.flush();\n		this.out.close();\n	}\n\n}

p00001	1	import { Scanner } from "https://deno.land/x/arkts/mod.ts";\n\nconst sc = new Scanner(Deno.stdin);\nconst array = new Array<number>(10);\nfor (let i = 0; i < array.length; i++) {\n  array[i] = sc.nextInt();\n}\narray = mergeSort(array);\nfor (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n  console.log(array[i]);\n}\n\nfunction mergeSort(array: Array<number>): Array<number> {\n  if (array.length <= 1) {\n    return array;\n  }\n  const head = split(array, 0, array.length / 2);\n  const tail = split(array, array.length / 2, array.length);\n  return merge(mergeSort(head), mergeSort(tail));\n}\n\nfunction split(array: Array<number>, start: number, end: number): Array<number> {\n  const newArray = new Array<number>(end - start);\n  for (let i = start, j = 0; i < end; i++, j++) {\n    newArray[j] = array[i];\n  }\n  return newArray;\n}\n\nfunction merge(a: Array<number>, b: Array<number>): Array<number> {\n  const merged = new Array<number>(a.length + b.length);\n  for (let i = 0, j = 0, k = 0; i < a.length + b.length; i++) {\n    if (j == a.length || k == b.length) {\n      merged[i] = j == a.length ? b[k++] : a[j++];\n    } else {\n      merged[i] = a[j] > b[k] ? a[j++] : b[k++];\n    }\n  }\n  return merged;\n}

p00001	1	import java.io.*;\nimport java.util.*;\nimport java.math.BigInteger;\n\npublic class Main{\n	\n	public static void main(String[] args){\n		try{\n\n			final int INF = Integer.MAX_VALUE, MINF = Integer.MIN_VALUE;\n			SpecialComparator scomparator = new SpecialComparator();\n			Scanner s = new Scanner(System.in);\n\n			\n			int[] in = new int[10];\n			for(int i = 0; i < 10; i++) in[i] = s.nextInt();\n			Arrays.sort(in);\n			for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) debugl(in[9-i]);\n\n		}catch(Exception e){\n			e.printStackTrace();\n		}\n	}\n\n	\n\n	private static void debugl(Object obj){\n		System.out.println(obj);\n	}\n	private static void debug(Object obj){\n		System.out.print(obj);\n	}\n	private static String toStrmd(Object[] obj){\n		return Arrays.deepToString(obj);\n	}\n}\n\nclass SpecialComparator implements Comparator{\n	private int index = 0;\n	public void setIndex(int index){\n		this.index = index;\n	}\n	public int compare(Object a, Object b){\n		String[] sa = (String[])a;\n		String[] sb = (String[])b;\n		return (sa[index].compareTo(sb[index]));\n	}\n}

p00001	5	import {\n  debug,\n  debugl,\n  Scanner,\n  toStrmd,\n} from "@std/io";\n\nimport {\n  Arrays,\n  Comparator,\n  Integer,\n} from "@std/math";\n\nconst INF = Number.MAX_VALUE;\nconst MINF = Number.MIN_VALUE;\n\nclass SpecialComparator implements Comparator {\n  index = 0;\n\n  setIndex(index: number) {\n    this.index = index;\n  }\n\n  compare(a: any, b: any) {\n    const sa = a as string[];\n    const sb = b as string[];\n    return sa[this.index].localeCompare(sb[this.index]);\n  }\n}\n\nconst scomparator = new SpecialComparator();\nconst s = new Scanner(process.stdin);\n\nconst in = new Array(10);\nfor (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) in[i] = s.nextInt();\nArrays.sort(in);\nfor (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) debugl(in[9 - i]);

p00001	1	import java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\n\npublic class Main {\n	public static void main(String[] args) throws IOException{\n	    BufferedReader br=new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in));\n	    int i;\n		int [] mountain;\n		int escape = 0;\n\n		mountain = new int[10];\n		\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n		\n\n\n\n\n\n		for(i=0;i<10;i++)\n	        mountain[i]=Integer.parseInt(br.readLine());\n\n		\n		for(int a = 0; a < 9; a++) {\n		    for(int b = a + 1; b < 10; b++) {\n		    	if(mountain[a] < mountain[b]) {\n		    		escape = mountain[b];\n					mountain[b] = mountain[a];\n					mountain[a] = escape;\n				}\n		    }\n		    \n		}\n\n		\n		System.out.println(mountain[0]);\n        System.out.println(mountain[1]);\n        System.out.println(mountain[2]);\n\n	}\n}

p00001	1	import java.util.Random;\nimport java.util.Scanner;\n\npublic class Main {\n	public static void main(String[] args) {\n		Scanner in = new Scanner(System.in);\n\n		int[] heights = new int[10];\n\n		for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n			heights[i] = in.nextInt();\n		}\n		\n		quicksort(heights, 0, heights.length - 1);\n		\n		for (int i = heights.length - 1; i >= heights.length - 3; i--) {\n			System.out.println(heights[i]);\n		}\n	}\n\n	private static void quicksort(int[] data, int start, int end) {\n		if (start > end) {\n			return;\n		}\n\n		int index = partition(data, start, end);\n		quicksort(data, start, index - 1);\n		quicksort(data, index + 1, end);\n	}\n\n	private static int partition(int[] data, int start, int end) {\n		int index = -1;\n		Random rd = new Random();\n\n		while (index < start || index > end) {\n			index = rd.nextInt(end + 1);\n		}\n\n		swap(data, index, end);\n		int pivot = data[end];\n\n		int i = -1, j = 0;\n\n		for (; j < end; j++) {\n			if (data[j] <= pivot) {\n				swap(data, ++i, j);\n			}\n		}\n		\n		swap(data, ++i, end);\n		\n		return i;\n	}\n\n	private static void swap(int[] data, int i, int j) {\n		int tmp = data[i];\n		data[i] = data[j];\n		data[j] = tmp;\n	}\n}
p00001	2	import { Scanner } from "https://deno.land/x/std@0.177.0/io/mod.ts";\n\nconst in = new Scanner(Deno.stdin);\n\nconst heights = new Array<number>(10);\n\nfor (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n  heights[i] = in.nextInt();\n}\n\nquicksort(heights, 0, heights.length - 1);\n\nfor (let i = heights.length - 1; i >= heights.length - 3; i--) {\n  console.log(heights[i]);\n}\n\nfunction quicksort(data: Array<number>, start: number, end: number): void {\n  if (start > end) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const index = partition(data, start, end);\n  quicksort(data, start, index - 1);\n  quicksort(data, index + 1, end);\n}\n\nfunction partition(data: Array<number>, start: number, end: number): number {\n  let index = -1;\n  const rd = new Random();\n\n  while (index < start || index > end) {\n    index = rd.nextInt(end + 1);\n  }\n\n  swap(data, index, end);\n  const pivot = data[end];\n\n  let i = -1, j = 0;\n\n  for (; j < end; j++) {\n    if (data[j] <= pivot) {\n      swap(data, ++i, j);\n    }\n  }\n  \n  swap(data, ++i, end);\n  \n  return i;\n}\n\nfunction swap(data: Array<number>, i: number, j: number): void {\n  const tmp = data[i];\n  data[i] = data[j];\n  data[j] = tmp;\n}

p00001	1	import java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\nimport java.util.ArrayDeque;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.Stack;\nimport java.util.Scanner;\nimport java.util.Queue;\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nclass Main{\n	static final boolean DEBUG = false;\n	static Scanner sc = new Scanner(new InputStreamReader(System.in));\n	static BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in));\n	\n	public static void main(String args[]) throws Exception{\n		int[] m = new int[10];\n		for(int i = 0; i < 10; i++) m[i] = readInt();\n		Arrays.sort(m);\n		for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) p(m[9-i]);\n	}\n\n	public static String readLine() throws IOException{return br.readLine();}\n	public static int readInt(){return sc.nextInt();}\n	public static void pa(Object[] arr){System.out.println(Arrays.toString(arr));}\n	public static void pa(int[] arr){System.out.println(Arrays.toString(arr));}\n	public static void pa(double[] arr){System.out.println(Arrays.toString(arr));}\n	public static void pa(boolean[] arr){System.out.println(Arrays.toString(arr));}\n	public static void p(Object o){System.out.println(o.toString());}\n	public static void d(Object o){if(DEBUG)System.out.println(o.toString());}\n	public static int[] parseInt(String[] arr){\n		int[] res = new int[arr.length];\n		for(int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)res[i] = Integer.parseInt(arr[i]);\n		return res;\n	}\n	public static int parseInt(Object o){return Integer.parseInt(o.toString());};\n}\n\nclass MyHashMap<E> extends HashMap<E, Integer>{\n	ArrayList<E> keyArray = new ArrayList<E>();\n	public void add(E key){add(key, 1);}\n	public void add(E key, Integer value){\n		if(containsKey(key)){\n			value += get(key);\n		}else{\n			keyArray.add(key);\n		}\n		put(key, value);\n	}\n}

p00001	1	import java.util.*;\npublic class Main {\n	public static void main(String[] args) {\n		Scanner sc = new Scanner(System.in);\n		List<Integer> list = new ArrayList<Integer>();\n		for(int i=0; i<10; i++) {\n			list.add(Integer.parseInt(sc.nextLine()));\n		}\n		Collections.sort(list, new Comparator<Integer>() {\n			@Override\n			public int compare(Integer first, Integer second) {\n				int f = first;\n				int s = second;\n				return s-f;\n			}\n		});\n		for(int i=0; i<3; i++) {\n			System.out.println(list.get(i));\n		}\n	}\n}

p00001	1	import java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Scanner;\n\npublic class Main {\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        List<Integer> list = new ArrayList<Integer>();\n        Scanner scanner = new Scanner(System.in);\n        while (scanner.hasNext()) {\n            list.add(Integer.parseInt(scanner.next()));\n        }\n        Collections.sort(list);\n        Collections.reverse(list);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n            System.out.println(list.get(i));\n        }\n    }\n}

p00001	1	import java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\n\n class Main {\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in));\n        String str;\n        ArrayList<Integer> al = new ArrayList<Integer>();\n        try {\n            while((str = br.readLine()) != null) {\n                al.add( Integer.parseInt(str) );\n            }\n        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        } catch (IOException e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        }\n        Collections.sort(al);\n        Collections.reverse(al);\n        for(int i=0; i<3; i++) {\n            System.out.println(al.get(i));\n        }\n    }\n}

p00002	1	class Main{\n  public void run(InputStream in,PrintStream out){\n    Scanner sc = new Scanner(in);\n\n    int a, b, i, j, dig;\n    for(;sc.hasNextInt();){\n\n      a = sc.nextInt();\n      b = sc.nextInt();\n\n\n      dig = caldig(a + b);\n      System.out.println(dig);\n    }\n    sc.close();\n  }\n  public static void main(String[] args){\n    (new Main()).run(System.in,System.out);\n  }\n\n  private static int caldig(int n){\n    int i;\n    for(i = 1;i < 10;i++){\n      if(n / 10 == 0)return i;\n      n = n / 10;\n    }\n    return 0;\n  }\n}

p00002	2	import { FastScanner, Methods } from "./fastscanner.ts";\nimport { PrintWriter } from "./printwriter.ts";\n\nfunction solve(in: FastScanner, out: PrintWriter, ms: Methods) {\n  while (in.hasNext()) {\n    out.println(String.valueOf(in.nextInt() + in.nextInt()).length());\n  }\n}\n\nfunction main() {\n  const main = new Main();\n  const in = new FastScanner(System.in);\n  const out = new PrintWriter(System.out);\n  const ms = new Methods();\n  main.solve(in, out, ms);\n  in.close();\n  out.close();\n}\n\nclass Main {\n  solve(in: FastScanner, out: PrintWriter, ms: Methods) {\n    solve(in, out, ms);\n  }\n}\n\nclass Methods {\n  print(...ar: any[]) {\n    console.log(ar);\n  }\n\n  yesno(out: PrintWriter, b: boolean) {\n    out.println(b ? "Yes" : "No");\n  }\n\n  YESNO(out: PrintWriter, b: boolean) {\n    out.println(b ? "YES" : "NO");\n  }\n\n  max(...ar: number[]) {\n    ar.sort((a, b) => a - b);\n    return ar[ar.length - 1];\n  }\n\n  min(...ar: number[]) {\n    ar.sort((a, b) => a - b);\n    return ar[0];\n  }\n\n  gcd(a: number, b: number) {\n    return b > 0 ? this.gcd(b, a % b) : a;\n  }\n\n  lcm(a: number, b: number) {\n    return (a / this.gcd(a, b)) * b;\n  }\n\n  isPrime(n: number) {\n    if (n == 2) return true;\n    if (n < 2 || n % 2 == 0) return false;\n    const d = Math.sqrt(n);\n    for (let i = 3; i <= d; i += 2) if (n % i == 0) {\n      return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n  }\n}\n\nclass FastScanner {\n  private in: InputStream;\n  private buffer: number[] = new Array(1024);\n  private length: number = 0;\n  private p: number = 0;\n\n  constructor(stream: InputStream) {\n    this.in = stream;\n  }\n\n  hasNextByte() {\n    if (this.p < this.length) return true;\n    else {\n      this.p = 0;\n      try {\n        this.length = this.in.read(this.buffer);\n      } catch (e) {\n        e.printStackTrace();\n      }\n      if (this.length <= 0) return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  readByte() {\n    if (this.hasNextByte() == true) return this.buffer[this.p++];\n    return -1;\n  }\n\n  isPrintable(n: number) {\n    return 33 <= n && n <= 126;\n  }\n\n  skip() {\n    while (this.hasNextByte() && !this.isPrintable(this.buffer[this.p])) this.p++;\n  }\n\n  hasNext() {\n    this.skip();\n    return this.hasNextByte();\n  }\n\n  next() {\n    if (!this.hasNext()) throw new Error("NoSuchElementException");\n    const sb = new StringBuilder();\n    let t = this.readByte();\n    while (this.isPrintable(t)) {\n      sb.appendCodePoint(t);\n      t = this.readByte();\n    }\n    return sb.toString();\n  }\n\n  nextArray(n: number) {\n    const ar = new Array(n);\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ar[i] = this.next();\n    return ar;\n  }\n\n  nextInt() {\n    return this.nextLong();\n  }\n\n  nextIntArray(n: number) {\n    const ar = new Array(n);\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ar[i] = this.nextInt();\n    return ar;\n  }\n\n  nextLong() {\n    if (!this.hasNext()) throw new Error("NoSuchElementException");\n    let minus = false;\n    let temp = this.readByte();\n    if (temp == "-".charCodeAt(0)) {\n      minus = true;\n      temp = this.readByte();\n    }\n    if (temp < "0".charCodeAt(0) || "9".charCodeAt(0) < temp) throw new Error("NumberFormatException");\n    let n = 0;\n    while (this.isPrintable(temp)) {\n      if ("0".charCodeAt(0) <= temp && temp <= "9".charCodeAt(0)) {\n        n *= 10;\n        n += temp - "0".charCodeAt(0);\n      } else throw new Error("NumberFormatException");\n      temp = this.readByte();\n    }\n    return minus ? -n : n;\n  }\n\n  nextLongArray(n: number) {\n    const ar = new Array(n);\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ar[i] = this.nextDouble();\n    return ar;\n  }\n\n  close() {\n    try {\n      this.in.close();\n    } catch (e) {}\n  }\n}\n\nclass PrintWriter {\n  private out: OutputStream;\n  private buffer: string[] = new Array(1024);\n  private length: number = 0;\n\n  constructor(stream: OutputStream) {\n    this.out = stream;\n  }\n\n  print(...ar: any[]) {\n    ar.forEach((a) => {\n      if (typeof a == "string") {\n        this.buffer.push(a);\n      } else if (typeof a == "number") {\n        this.buffer.push(String(a));\n      } else if (typeof a == "boolean") {\n        this.buffer.push(a ? "true" : "false");\n      } else if (typeof a == "object") {\n        this.buffer.push(JSON.stringify(a));\n      }\n    });\n    this.length += ar.length;\n    if (this.length >= this.buffer.length) {\n      this.flush();\n    }\n  }\n\n  println(...ar: any[]) {\n    this.print(...ar);\n    this.println();\n  }\n\n  println() {\n    this.buffer.push("\n");\n    this.length++;\n    if (this.length >= this.buffer.length) {\n      this.flush();\n    }\n  }\n\n  flush() {\n    try {\n      this.out.write(this.buffer.join("").getBytes());\n    } catch (e) {\n      e.printStackTrace();\n    }\n    this.buffer = new Array(1024);\n    this.length = 0;\n  }\n\n  close() {\n    this.flush();\n    try {\n      this.out.close();\n    } catch (e) {}\n  }\n}\n\nmain();

i<n; i++) ar[i] = this.nextInt();\n		return ar;\n	}\n	\n	public nextLong () {\n		if (!this.hasNext()) throw new NoSuchElementException();\n		let minus = false;\n		let temp = this.readByte();\n		if (temp == '-') {\n			minus = true;\n			temp = this.readByte();\n		}\n		if (temp<'0' || '9'<temp) throw new NumberFormatException();\n		let n = 0;\n		while (this.isPrintable(temp)) {\n			if ('0'<=temp && temp<='9') {\n				n *= 10;\n				n += temp - '0';\n			}\n			else throw new NumberFormatException();\n			temp = this.readByte();\n		}\n		return minus? -n : n;\n	}\n	\n	public nextLongArray (n: number) {\n		let ar = new Array(n);\n		for (let i=0; i<n; i++) ar[i] = this.nextLong();\n		return ar;\n	}\n	\n	public nextDouble () {\n		return Number(this.next());\n	}\n	\n	public nextDoubleArray (n: number) {\n		let ar = new Array(n);\n		for (let i=0; i<n; i++) ar[i] = this.nextDouble();\n		return ar;\n	}\n	\n	public close () {\n		try {this.in.close();}\n		catch(e){}\n	}\n	\n}\n\nexport class PrintWriter {\n	\n	private out: OutputStream;\n	\n	constructor (stream: OutputStream) {\n		this.out = stream;\n	}\n	\n	public print (...ar: any[]) {\n		ar.forEach(a => {\n			if (a instanceof Array) {\n				a.forEach(b => {\n					this.out.write(b.toString());\n				});\n			}\n			else {\n				this.out.write(a.toString());\n			}\n		});\n	}\n	\n	public println (...ar: any[]) {\n		this.print(...ar);\n		this.out.write("\n");\n	}\n	\n	public close () {\n		try {this.out.close();}\n		catch(e){}\n	}\n	\n}\n\nexport class InputStream {\n	\n	private buffer: number[] = new Array(1024);\n	private length: number = 0;\n	private p: number = 0;\n	\n	public read (buffer: number[]) {\n		return -1;\n	}\n	\n	public close () {}\n	\n}\n\nexport class OutputStream {\n	\n	public write (buffer: number[]) {}\n	\n	public close () {}\n	\n}\n\nexport class NoSuchElementException extends Error {}\n\nexport class NumberFormatException extends Error {}
